OPENUNSEAL (ENTSIEGELUNGSKARTIERUNG) - THIRD-PARTY SOFTWARE & GEODATA NOTICES
================================================================================

This document records the third-party software packages, reproduced runtime
copyright/license notices (direct and transitive), native mobile SDKs,
runtime container components, and default geodata sources used by OpenUnseal.
Regenerate from package-lock.json and node_modules via:
  npm run licenses:generate

Project licenses: see LICENSE (AGPL-3.0-or-later), apps/mobile/LICENSE (MPL-2.0),
packages/shared/LICENSE (MPL-2.0), and LICENSING.md.

================================================================================
3. PREBUILT LIBVIPS & BUNDLED NATIVE LIBRARIES (@img/sharp-libvips-*)
================================================================================

sharp@0.35.5 (Apache-2.0) dynamically loads platform-specific @img/sharp-libvips-*
prebuilt binaries distributed under LGPL-3.0-or-later.
Note: Source distribution on GitHub and self-hosted network operation of apps/api do not
distribute prebuilt libvips binaries. If prebuilt Docker images bundling @img/sharp-libvips-*
are ever distributed publicly, accompany them with verbatim LGPL-3.0 and GPL-3.0 license texts
and relinking / corresponding source information per LGPL-3.0 Section 4.

# `@img/sharp-libvips-linux-x64`

Prebuilt libvips and dependencies for use with sharp on Linux (glibc) x64.
